The proposed blanket exclusion when both caps are present required correction. With A0=36*p*a0/960, R=14*p*(n0+n1)/960 and r=min(n0,n1)/max(n0,n1), the proposed bound -A0+2RY equals p*a0*(28r-8)/960. It is negative only for r<2/7, which has not been established at the actual law. The exact maximum is the largest of the diagonal score B=-A0+SY and the two bounded axis scores. Both axis scores are strictly negative, so the maximum's sign is exactly B's. The necessary lex-optimal diagonal-price inequality is strict; equality gives an original full-vector lex descent. A failed upper-bound shortcut is not a realizable native counterexample.

across j. All marked/labeled records, newborn bits and ties remain.

fixed U, the least available W_j is max(0,max_{m in N_j}(D_j-U_m));
unavailable W_j instead requires this maximum to vanish. The exact
minimum is attained among at most nine U pairs in {0,D_0,D_1} squared,
with explicit availability discards and recovered finite original slopes.

segment or point by demand levels and the diagonal proves completeness.

1 October 2026, OBSCURED-LOCATION. RI310 is independently accepted as an exact
conditional theorem for its entire assigned sign-restricted family:

The complete objective gain is
G=-A0+Q*(x0+x1)+R*(y0+y1)-B*z,
with A0=16*Cw*a0, Q=4*Cw*(t0+t1), R=Cw*(56/9)*(n0+n1),

mu increments in tight groups and setting z=0 improves the gain by
at least 64*aChi*Q*z*(10/3-|T|), strictly when z>0. The replacement
may require a smaller finite step because another mu cap was slack.
This proves equality of the full and reduced suprema and equivalence
of nonnegative-gain finite-direction existence; it is not a global theorem.

Both tight mu groups leave two exact nu capacities. A blocked column
has capacity zero; an unblocked column has a0/max_(l in A_j)n_l,
or no directional bound when A_j is empty. With one tight mu group,

## RI-26 conditional kinematic and record result

The supplied exponential flat-FLRW radial metric, comoving clocks and null/
geometric-optics propagation give q_r=q_e−H a_star ell/c. Positive q_r is a
unique finite arrival with frequency ratio q_r/q_e; expansion q_r=0 is only
asymptotic and q_r<0 is beyond the supplied model's future reach. Neither
nonfinite case gets a receipt, measured zero frequency or infinite timestamp.
All signs, chronology and inclusive cutoffs are rational; static H=0 has
separate rational time. Future scheduled emissions after cutoff are refused
as invalid actual inputs, while in-window emissions remain even without a
visible receipt. Finite absence is not a horizon observation.

The note proves order, a_r nu_r=a_e nu_e, coordinate-rescaling and ideal local-
duration offset invariance, and the fixed-emission-time static limit. These
are standard supplied kinematics, not new energy laws or Einstein dynamics.
One redshift does not determine geometry: (H,d_e/c)=(1,1/2) and (2,1/4) give
z=1 with delays ln2 and (ln2)/2. Their full timing records are not equated.
The finite two-pulse secant ln2/ln(4/3) lies strictly between its endpoint
instantaneous stretches 2 and 3; it is not either instantaneous redshift.

Records retain distinct emission/signal/crest identities, transmitted and
received reference payloads, actual local times/frequencies, local precursors
and finite closure. H/q/coordinates/offsets/forecasts remain separate audit.
Rational logarithm enclosures and exact grid/upward-error rounding prove the
synthetic display bounds, not physical calibration. Ambiguous positive timing
gaps refuse a ratio. Rounded differences need not be offset-invariant, and
unknown cross-observer offsets prevent flight-time inference from transmitted
timestamp subtraction. Whole-prefix clock-interval feasibility uses a running
maximum lower endpoint; pairwise checking was rejected by counterexample.

Receiver-only inference imports no geometry/simulator. Absent preannounced
IDs get only `not_received_by_cutoff`, not proof of emission or eternal
nonarrival. Common emitter clock/epoch, unit rates, pulse train, truthful
reference payload and ideal calibration are supplied premises. Matching
strings or immutable objects do not authenticate them. Existing records are
not modified by later interpretation; rendering failures refuse the run rather
than hiding an exact receipt behind an absence report.

## RI-22 proved alternative and the missing premise

Take D=R+iJ with R real symmetric PSD, J arbitrary real antisymmetric, and
total-entry mass one. Define D star E=R tensor S+i(J tensor S+R tensor K).
The note proves closure across carrier sizes, all-Boolean weak positivity,
normalization, associativity, swap/factor-permutation covariance, unit [1],
ambient real bilinearity and normalized separate affinity. It retains both
whole-carrier complex marginals and scalar rectangular-event independence.
Separate real congruence/incidence maps commute with composition on the
unnormalized cones; partition maps preserve mass but are not the only maps
that can do so. Generic overlapping/real maps can change or annihilate mass.

The class contains every individually normalized complex PSD kernel and the
fixed signed seed, but also non-PSD Di. Even two individual PSD projectors
can have a non-PSD star joint; no ordinary-QM composite subtheory is preserved.
Complex bilinearity is not an exclusion: on arbitrary complex matrices the
extension is A star B=A tensor B-A_a tensor B_a, A_a=(A-A^T)/2.

Ordinary tensor composition contains the missing -J tensor K term. The exact
rectangle event-pair correction is +J(A,B)K(C,F); scalar diagonal independence
does not see it. Full event-pair multiplicativity on singleton rectangles,
together with biadditivity, fixes every ordinary tensor entry. That narrow
sufficiency is proved, but the stronger premise is not derived from DET.

Under star powers, every scalar event depends only on R^tensor(m). The
unchanged RI-21 parity event with its intact real seed weighs m!det(R_I),
not m!det(D_I). For Di this is +1/2 rather than ordinary tensor's -3/2.
This changes the accepted tensor-law premise; RI-20/21 are not falsified.

## RI-22 operational and source boundaries

For n>=2 the raw cone has lineality ±iJ. All nonnegative real-linear scalar
functionals annihilate it and factor through R. All formal Boolean base
weights determine R, but neither they nor the real-map/star catalogue see J.
Formal complex marginals preserve information without supplying a scalar
observation. Mass is not faithful. If effects must also obey 0<=f<=M on the
whole cone, they reduce to f=aM: bounded effects do not separate even all R.
The seed has a Boolean weight four at mass one, so those weights are not
automatically probabilities in one recorded partition.

An exact rational complex unitary sends Di to a mass-one matrix with
singleton weight -23/50. Hence arbitrary complex congruence availability
would exclude this class. A pointed/separating-effect premise, all-complex-
direction positivity tests or full event-pair multiplication are other
stronger requirements; their distinct conclusions and missing justifications
are explicit. Complex bilinearity alone is insufficient. Existing H/P/Z lifts
and one tensor/coupler/read interface do not grant those operations globally
on this different pre-PSD class. PairKernel.compose and every accepted QR
instrument remain unchanged. This is a countermodel only to the listed weaker
premises, not a replacement physical theory or global axiom adoption.

## RI-21 finite-copy result and exact resource boundary

For n×n Hermitian total-entry-normalized weak-positive D, supply the single
fixed seed b=(1,-1,1), B=bb†, with total-entry mass one and trace three.
For each principal subset I of size m, use its m! distinct permutation
histories, paired with seed marker 0 for even permutations and 1 for odd.
Marker reuse is legal because the candidate tuples are distinct; the unused
third seed coordinate stays in the carrier for normalization. The exact
Boolean-event weight is m!det(D_I), by direct permutation reindexing.
Padding unused candidate factors by their whole carriers preserves that value.

The note proves the complete principal-minor criterion and both directions
of the resulting finite catalogue theorem: all 2^n-1 nonempty-subset events
on D^tensor(n) tensor B are nonnegative iff D is PSD. The event definitions
do not depend on D's values. At most n candidate copies and one seed suffice;
this is not a minimality, dimension-independent, runtime or sample bound.
Zero minors/event weights pass, and n=1 is included. The standard determinant
identity and Dowker–Wilkes permutation machinery are explicitly credited;
no literature novelty or DET-native admission theorem is claimed.

All principal subsets matter: the original D3=(J3-I3)/6 has negative pair
events -1/18 but a positive full determinant. Conversely
H=[[1,1,1],[1,1,-1],[1,-1,1]]/5 is weak positive with all order-two minors
zero and third-order event -24/125. The complex Di pair-plus-seed event
is -3/2. Zero direct-sum D3 has every leading minor zero yet nonleading
negative pair events. Diagonal/singular PSD and n=1 controls are included.

There is no conflict with RI-20's finite fixed linear-menu obstruction:
these tests repeat D and are nonlinear in the original candidate. On a
composite space the unknown is restricted to the tensor-power image.
Real seed signs plus D-dependent copies also avoid the one-copy fixed-real-
probe premise. D3 still passes all self-powers without the signed seed.
The specified seed is stronger admission data than a generic non-R+ member;
its copy bound cannot be assigned to every such resource.

Identical independent candidate preparation, independent seed admission,
the tensor law and nonnegativity of the listed composite events remain
supplied candidate premises. Equality of residual matrices does not imply
independence, cloning access or merged origins. Excluding a seed coordinate
from an event does not prepare a truncated seed. Catalogue events overlap
and are not automatically a recorded partition or available sharp operations.
The (R,Z) distinction and full retained histories remain intact. No global
axiom, full-QM/complex-field selection or native F/L is established.

## RI-20 result and premise decision

The note credits Dowker–Wilkes Theorem 4: a tensor-closed class of weak-positive
systems lies in S or R+. One admitted negative/nonreal event-pair resource
excludes R+, giving containment in S, not equality. All-S probes and Galois
self-duality are not needed for this containment inference. Boës–Navascués
Lemmas 2–3 provide the distinct finite-copy limit and tailored quantum-probe
route. A positive off-diagonal entry alone does not exclude R+.

For fixed independently specified PSD probes B and Boolean event incidence R,
the exact linear test is Tr(DT) with T=(RBR^T)^T. Including every base
indicator, the closed conic hull K of these T forces all normalized Hermitian
test-positive D to be PSD iff K=PSD_n. A proper K has a non-PSD separator D0;
adding sufficiently small delta I makes total-entry mass positive while
retaining a negative eigenvalue, allowing legal normalization. The proof
keeps the complex transpose, closure, base-event and unrestricted-candidate
hypotheses explicit. It does not apply to a restricted source cone unchanged.

Finite fixed matrix/event menus are polyhedral and inadequate for n>=2.
Candidate-dependent self-powers are nonlinear tests, outside that argument;
a finite generating alphabet need not give finitely many realized tests.
The rank-one appended-coordinate helper supplies every vv†, including
zero-sum v, when arbitrary complex amplitudes are admitted. Four phases with
continuous magnitudes and grouping can also suffice. These are mathematical
resource constructions, not preparation or apparatus-availability theorems.

Both countermodels remain: (J3-I3)/6 is weak positive, non-PSD and valid at
every self-power; the matrix with diagonal 1/2 and off-diagonals i,-i passes
all real PSD probes but fails its own square with event weight -3/2.
The second is not a counterexample to unbounded tensor-closed-class selection.

The repository audit finds PSD explicitly assumed by the pair-kernel and
strict derivation. Fixed reference/coupler/recordability interfaces do not
establish pre-PSD all-Boolean tensor closure. A particular admitted PSD
interference resource is not itself circular; its admission and class-wide
closure still need independent justification. A postcoupler residual can
be PSD yet not exactly recordable. Faithful process probes and faithful
normalization mass do not imply the missing event-test resource richness.
The full (R,Z) distinction and retained pair-kernel payload are preserved.

The smallest unproved arrow is therefore a pre-PSD admission/composition
contract: specify the actual class and justify its event positivity,
independent product, arbitrary finite mutual/self closure and one admitted
resource outside R+. If unproved, these stay additional candidate premises.
No global axiom is adopted. Full QM, complex-field selection, purification,
local tomography, instrument availability and native F/L remain separate.

## RI-19 proved results and exact boundary

For rank-one Q, p=e_Q(d) and c=u+iv, every calibrated positive real-linear
full-C→C0 branch is uniquely J2(pH+uK+vL). H,K,L have traces 1,0,0.
The projected input cone is exactly p≥2|c|; positivity is equivalent to
H+(cos(theta)K+sin(theta)L)/2≥0 for every theta. The uniform defect condition
is exactly a+sqrt(b²+e²)/2≤epsilon, where a,b,e are the I-Q expectations
of H,K,L. These are whole-domain symbolic equivalences, not grid tests.
Branchwise Phi descent is exactly K=L=0; epsilon=0 uniquely recovers Q.

Small error is not exact admission: the positive C→C near-ideal fixed target
with output c=epsilon exp(i theta)/2 has raw error 2epsilon p but nonzero
polarized joint cross forms ±3epsilon exp(i theta)/10. A positive ready path
is inadmissible; no projection, reset or recoupling repairs it. Conversely,
lawful C→C0 approximate branches can retain dependence on both input-c
coordinates. Exact output eligibility does not certify typed interchange
or supplied physical availability.

The unchanged fixed cut/empty-A/tilted-read suffix is positive, mass-preserving
and rank four on arbitrary Hermitian branch payloads. Its probability-row
minor is -27/78125. With r retained, for beta>0 the complete initial-law rank
is exactly 2+rank{(K0,K1),(L0,L1)}, hence two, three or four; at beta=0 it is
two. The ideal RI-18 rank-two theorem is unchanged. Initial X/Z remain
invisible in the classified form, so even rank four is not full tomography.
An admitted epsilon=1/5 family distinguishes same-Phi sources c=±1/8 and
c=±i/8 with full-law TV 12beta/125 and 7beta/50, respectively.

For a fixed explicitly input/r-independent complete reference law, common
observed metadata and both exactly calibrated branches, the scalar-law error
from the ideal is at most beta sum_r p_r sqrt(epsilon_r), hence beta sqrt(epsilon).
Two normalized same-Y inputs obey the stronger cap beta min(1,2sqrt(epsilon)).
Branch-specific and homogeneous incoming-mass versions are proved separately.
Contraction is from C0's base/raw-trace norm directly to the complete scalar
law, never an assumed raw-trace contraction on native L_t. At beta=0 only
the same two calibrated scalar stops remain, even if audit prefixes differ.
No sharp composed constant or finite-sample precision is claimed.

## RI-16 result and load-bearing premises

Use one complete stationary experiment or finite controller on a finite direct
sum of finite-dimensional closed pointed generating cones with faithful mass.
Mutually exclusive silent/committing branch maps include policy selection;
independently available commands are not simply added as outcomes. Known
context, full prior prefix, initial pending word, nominal policy and residual
types are retained. Hidden branches aggregate only inside the same complete
retained-label/output-type coordinate, without making hidden paths observable.

The countable output is a complete l1 direct sum over actual words and
committing labels in faithful output base norms. Positive output norm equals
total output mass, uniformly across all words. The chronological prefix-shift
recurrence is

    H_0 = 0,    H_(N+1) = J_B + sum_a L_a H_N S_a.

Optional empty-token steps really retain no letter; N counts total silent
steps, not just retained-word length. The proof constructs a positive full
operator-norm limit H and a positive never functional ell, with

    vH + ell = u,    ell S = ell,
    ||H-H_N|| = ||u S^N-ell||,
    ||(H-H_N)x|| = u S^N x-ell(x)  for x >= 0.

The right-hand operator error uses the input base dual norm. The output norm
uses the complete retained residual sum. No convergence of S^N, silent
residual state or inverse of I-S is needed. Countable output mass plus never
probability is complete; never is not an observed null record or a supplied
terminal residual. Convergence is uniform over bounded inputs of each fixed
model, not across policies/model families or growing prefixes.

H solves the prefix-shift recurrence and is its least positive bounded
solution. Without empty-token steps, recursive word coefficients make it
unique. A genuine hidden empty-step dark component admits larger positive,
even mass-nonincreasing fixed points that fabricate never commitment; only
the least solution is the actual law. The old unlabelled equation H=B+HS
does not define this retained-word object.

Uniformly bounded word-dependent linear continuations interchange with the
limit while retaining the original labels. Positive mass-nonincreasing maps
are natural base-norm contractions. Dropping that uniform bound, using
nonuniform coordinate norms, erasing words, or allowing infinite input
dimension has an explicit counterexample. Hidden branch identities and
discarded empty-step counts cannot control a retained-word continuation.
Only positive total branch weight permits normalization and an append;
zero-weight branches and never probability supply no conditional state.

I/J's positive terminal effects may enter only as scalar outputs with full
word/cell/sign labels. A pre-read audit does not repair the nonlinearity of
N -> e(N) N/m(N) or supply a postread residual. Existing literal-cut maps
remain legitimate linear residual outputs on their declared return type.
The small nonclassical supplement uses that old cut-face representation;
its terminal scalar helper is only a labeled mathematical continuation
statistic, not a new instrument or operational reuse after a terminal read.

## Consolidated result and cross-bundle boundaries

The synthesis presents G/I/J as one self-contained conditional theorem.
It supplies the actual sixteen-label image and inverse/filter, differentiates
native mass from raw trace, and states the known finite common-X A matrix
and its repeated use with an exactly calibrated tilted terminal read.
Three full eight-outcome setting tables at the same normalized snapshot
characterize compatible positive sources and reconstruct every native entry.
The same object obeys sharp filtered-state bounds and the native trace-norm
comparison, with radical constants distinguished from rational enclosures.
A supplied exact feasible candidate proves nonempty deterministic budget
feasibility and a diameter bound; a failed candidate remains inconclusive.
Typed States alone bind matched metadata; raw audits require metadata=None.
Terminal-only semantics and unknown earlier selection probabilities remain.

The finite proof needs neither B/inverse commands, an all-real continuous
group, Hamiltonian classification nor RI-07. Literal cuts are needed only
for the optional larger finite-policy corollary. H's stronger reversible
group result is a separate classification proposition, not a missing axiom
silently imported into three-setting reconstruction.

The route audit separates whole-domain mass-dominated effects on K_P,
the control-selected local C, its proper catalogue-relative qubit quotient,
direct polarized C0 viability, RI-08c's conditional record-producing branch,
the fixed equal-block K_t image, RI-15's lossless retained-context wrapper,
and the explicit mathematical cut closure L_t. Each arrow is labeled by
its restriction/construction, additional-premise or implementation status.
There is no single implemented probability- and type-complete operational
chain: earlier branch/reference-selection weights still need orchestration,
and physical preparation/control availability is not derived. Earlier C
states remain legitimate; J2Phi is not identity and terminal wrappers are
not retagged reusable.

Sharpness, blind-axis conditioning/rank loss, bounded endpoint dark-state,
rare-cell conditional and paired unknown-calibration examples remain intact.
The final two open bridges are precise: RI-07's finite FULL labels do not
cover arbitrarily long retained command words, requiring a word-indexed
output norm, convergence, mass/label accounting and word-dependent
continuation argument; and exact/synthetic comparator work does not supply
a measured pilot's data, target/tolerance, calibrated noise/stability,
measured costs, frozen conventional baseline and held-out evaluation.
Neither bridge is implemented. J budgets are not confidence levels and its
cone-specific constants do not automatically transfer to the comparator.

## RI-08j artifacts and result

New isolated bundle:
[OBSERVATION_STABILITY.md](../validation/t8-q-observation-stability-2026-09-13/OBSERVATION_STABILITY.md),
[model.py](../validation/t8-q-observation-stability-2026-09-13/model.py),
[check.py](../validation/t8-q-observation-stability-2026-09-13/check.py).

Retain the accepted interior L_t, native sixteen-label kernel, fixed known
common-X commands and one calibrated tilted terminal read. Sources are
normalized by native mass, not ordinary trace, and compared at the same
known context, full earlier prefix and initial pending word. Full laws
retain all eight cell/sign outcomes at resolved settings empty/A/AA.
The proof allows general fixed interior t; the primary executor remains
the accepted t=3/5 fixture. Diagnostics do not add available operations.

Write epsilon_k=TV(p_k,p'_k),
d_F=(1/2) sum_alpha ||sigma_alpha-sigma'_alpha||_1 and
d_N=(1/2)||N-N'||_1. For V's three unit backward-axis rows and
B=V^(-1), let b_k=||B_column_k||_2. The full-domain theorem is

    max epsilon_k <= d_F <= sum b_k epsilon_k <= C max epsilon_k,
    C=sum b_k.

The proof uses ||Delta sigma||_1=max(|Delta q|,||Delta r||_2), the
corresponding binary-pair TV identity and the inverse-column triangle
bound. Three separate positive cells with opposite small Bloch vectors
along the three inverse columns attain the weighted sum. Equal errors
attain C, a single active cell attains each coefficient, and a transfer
of center mass attains the lower coefficient one. These are full-domain
mathematical witnesses, not physically available preparation claims.

At the accepted tilt,
b0=b2=125 sqrt(41)/768 and b1=5 sqrt(6409)/384, giving
C approximately 3.126749168936497. The decimal is display-only.
The executable certifies exact rational upper bounds by squaring, with
bhat0=bhat2=640313/614400 and bhat1=1000703/960000.
The sum of these slightly larger safe bounds is 8004483/2560000;
sharpness belongs to the radical coefficients, not their enclosures.

For a general axis with a=|n_x|>0 and b=||n_perp||>0,
a^2+b^2=1, the note derives the inverse-column formula and proves
a*C -> 1 as a -> 0, and b*C -> 25/12 as b -> 0. Thus a full-rank
interior read can be arbitrarily poorly conditioned. At the exactly
blind axes the earlier rank-loss result applies instead. No directional
stability conclusion is inferred merely from a nonzero determinant.

The native tensor-image identity gives
||Delta N||_1=(1/4) sum ||Delta rho_alpha||_1, hence

    d_F/(1+|t|) <= d_N <= d_F/(1-|t|).

The endpoint factor is necessary even on bounded raw sources: move
Q_Z- between cells00 and10 and retain the same
(3+t)/(1+t) Q_Z- block in cell01. The normalized sources have
d_N=1/4 and d_F=epsilon_k=(1-t)/4. At t=1 a nonzero raw dark
difference is invisible to every whole-cone mass-dominated effect.
The lower factor also has an extreme-ray equality witness. The composed
C/(1-|t|) coefficient is not claimed jointly sharp; raw distance is
not a probability distance.

The new bounded audit accepts exact complete ordered tables y_k,
three deterministic nonnegative budgets eta_k, and one supplied positive
normalized candidate. A passing candidate proves nonempty feasibility
and bounds the feasible-set diameter by 2 sum bhat_k eta_k in d_F,
and by that number divided by 1-|t| in d_N. A failed candidate reports
only inconclusive failure of that candidate; it does not prove infeasibility.
No fitting, solver, clipping, projection, renormalization or table repair
is performed. Tables with inconsistent cell masses can still have a
budget-feasible center candidate while the unchanged exact inverse
properly refuses them. Conversely, an exact feasible source can coexist
with failure of a different candidate at zero budget.

Typed audits retain and match the exact source object, full known
context/history and initial pending word. Raw audits require metadata=None
and reject any attached context/history declaration through the common
audit validator. A caller requiring verified metadata binding must supply
a typed State; no raw matrix is promoted into a prepared State.
Success/failure objects are immutable
audits, not States, physical reads or postmeasurement continuations.
Direct construction revalidates rather than accepting forged conclusions;
zero cells and one-shot exact inputs are handled without cell division.

Rare-cell opposite X states have d_F=q and all three errors 3q/5,
but conditional trace distance one. A positive cell-mass floor is needed
for conditional bounds. A simultaneous common-X-commuting conjugation
of source and unknown read frame leaves the full laws unchanged:
deterministic budgets cannot establish calibration. The note's optional
linear-target corollary transfers event-probability errors only; a binary
expectation difference can incur 2 eta, not eta. No confidence level,
repeated-preparation access or application performance follows.

## RI-08i artifacts and result

New isolated bundle:

This conditional observation study retains the accepted interior L_t,
its full sixteen-label native kernel, fixed known common-X A/B/AB/inverse
commands and literal cuts. It adds exactly one explicitly available
calibrated terminal internal read. Positivity of its effects does not
derive availability or calibration from DET. The proof treats general
fixed interior t, whereas the executable is restricted to t=3/5.

For each independent cell, faithful filtered coordinates are
sigma=E^(1/2) rho E^(1/2)=(q I+x X+y Y+z Z)/2.
The terminal effects are Tr(Q_n,± sigma)=(q±n·r)/2, with the actual
cell (a,b) retained: eight full outcomes, not one global binary
outcome erasing cells. All weights are nonnegative and sum to native
normalization mass. The sole primary read uses n=(3/5,0,4/5) and
Q+=(1/10)[[9,3],[3,1]]. Z/X alternatives remain diagnostics.

The backward-effect rank is proved for all finite words, not inferred
from enumeration. Common-X words preserve the X line and YZ plane;
A alone supplies the necessary axes because its transverse cosine
and sine are -7/25 and 24/25. Per-cell rank is
1+[n_x nonzero]+2[(n_y,n_z) nonzero]. Thus:

| Read catalogue | Minimal mass-retaining homogeneous linear rank | Normalized affine dimension |
| --- | ---: | ---: |
| Z or another YZ-plane read | 12 | 11 |
| X or minus X read | 8 | 7 |
| Tilted read with both components | 16 | 15 |

The full-span lower bound is separate from the normalized-law theorem:
any homogeneous mass-retaining predictive summary has kernel within
the common backward-effect kernel, as can be tested with small positive
perturbations about an interior state. The displayed summaries attain
the ranks. No minimum for arbitrary nonlinear encodings or a physically
available preparation subset is inferred.

The Z quotient is four q>=sqrt(y²+z²) cones with positive section x=0;
the X quotient is four q>=|x| cones with section y=z=0. The tilted
summary is faithful on the entire native span. These positive linear
sections retain mass and respect commands/cuts, but do not supply a
physical reset, preparation or erasure of original residuals.

For normalized sources at the same context/calibration, full prior
committed prefix, initial pending word and nominal metadata, equality
of the respective summaries is equivalent to equality of ALL finite
common command/cut/stop/terminal-read outcome laws. Sufficiency is
finite policy-tree induction using descended operations/effects;
necessity supplies a separating empty/A/AA experiment when needed.
The policy may inspect retained nominal labels and committed outcomes,
not the unknown raw payload retained for audit. Stopping is not a
raw-state observation. Different actual words remain different records,
even when they implement the same matrix. Unnormalized equivalence
uses outcome-weight measures including mass, not normalized laws.

One-cell filtered Q_X+ and Q_X- are invisible to every common-X word
followed by a Z read, but the tilted plus weights are 4/5 and 1/5.
Adding a merely nonparallel control is insufficient: pi-Z conjugation
still preserves the X line and YZ observation plane under all words.
These are mathematical witnesses and a rejected criterion, not
available preparations or an extra primary control.

For the exact tilted read, resolved words empty/A/AA give backward
axes (3/5,0,4/5), (3/5,96/125,-28/125) and
(3/5,-1344/3125,-2108/3125), with determinant -73728/78125.
In each cell the full outcome differences d_k=p_k,+ - p_k,- yield
x=(125 d0+70 d1+125 d2)/192,
y=(-55 d0+180 d1-125 d2)/192 and
z=(195 d0-70 d1-125 d2)/256.
The shared cell sum is q, never a divisor.
At t=3/5 recover rho=10 D_b^(-1) sigma D_b^(-1), with
D0=diag(2,1) and D1=diag(1,2), then every native entry.

Exact three-setting tables are compatible with a unique normalized
source iff q is shared across settings, sum q=1, every q>=0, and
q²>=x²+y²+z². Zero q forces a zero block without division.
The executable refuses malformed/nonexact/reordered tables, inconsistent
cell sums and nonpositive reconstructed states, then exactly rechecks
every full table entry. It does not clip or fit incompatible input.
Three distinct resolved binary settings are minimal: with q known,
k settings span at most k Bloch axes per cell. Resolved randomized
selectors count as their actual settings; cuts add only q.

The new TerminalResult retains its exact pre-read State, complete
context and calibration, all earlier records, pending word, full
(a,b,sign) terminal record, declared effect and conditional snapshot
probability. It is not a State, has no postmeasurement residual, and

read availability or a physical preparation claim.

The tilted reconstruction establishes state observability under known
commands and exact probabilities, not identification of an unknown

## RI-08h artifacts and result

New isolated bundle:
[REVERSIBLE_GENERATOR.md](../validation/t8-q-reversible-generator-2026-09-13/REVERSIBLE_GENERATOR.md),
[model.py](../validation/t8-q-reversible-generator-2026-09-13/model.py),
[check.py](../validation/t8-q-reversible-generator-2026-09-13/check.py).

The classification is conditional on four new law premises on the actual
sixteen-dimensional span of fixed interior L_t: real-linearity and an
all-real group law, positive maps with positive inverses, preservation
of native normalization mass, and strong continuity. It is not a
physical time law or a DET derivation of those premises.

Faithful normalized extreme points form four disjoint Bloch spheres.
The identity-connected flow preserves each cell block, hence each
q_ab individually. Filtering by E_ab^(1/2), with
E_ab=(I+(-1)^b t Z)/4, makes that mass ordinary trace. An affine
Bloch-ball bijection fixes its unique center and is orthogonal.
The integral identity A_s J=integral_s^(s+a) A_v dv, with
J=integral_0^a A_u du invertible for small a, proves differentiability
and a constant skew generator without assuming them.

Each block therefore has the exact classified form
T_s(rho)=E^(-1/2) exp(-i s H) E^(1/2) rho E^(1/2)
exp(i s H) E^(-1/2), H Hermitian. The converse is proved.
Equivalently the generator acts as G rho+rho G†, with
G†E+EG=0 and G=-i E^(-1/2) H E^(1/2).
H is unique modulo a scalar I; four blocks give twelve nontrivial
real parameters and four trivial scalar gauge directions. The fixed
frame is retained. No parameter values, physical energies or rates
are identified. Raw trace need not be conserved by the weighted
congruences, although native mass is.

Every classified flow commutes with every literal cut and leaves
all four cell weights fixed. For normalized sources with the same
known context, full committed prefix, initial pending word and nominal
command history, a finite common retained-history command/cut/stop
policy has the same future cut-outcome law whenever the weights agree.
The same statement holds across candidate generator implementations
of the same nominal commands. Actually changing or omitting a known
command changes metadata and cannot be called equal full records.
Unequal initial pending words can also change a metadata-aware policy's
cut/stop choices. Existing records, full raw residuals, origin-tagged
precursors and ordered pending words remain intact. A command is
silent; a positive cut commits its actual word and clears only pending
commands. No command label is a physical duration.

The interior assumption is essential. At either polarized endpoint,
V_s=Q_bright+exp(s) Q_dark gives a positive reversible mass-preserving
group but unbounded positive raw dark growth; no faithful inverse
filter exists. Static transpose preserves positivity and the real
diagonal E mass while reversing Bloch orientation. It is NOT a
counterexample obtained by removing only continuity from an R-group:
det A_s=(det A_(s/2))^2=1, and a permutation homomorphism R→S4
is also trivial by divisibility. A choice-dependent additive function
with f(1)=0 and f(sqrt(2))=1 supplies the genuine discontinuous
reparameterization example, explicitly nonconstructive and not
executable. Forward filtered dephasing is positive, but its inverse
has a negative eigenvalue on a pure X input.

The typed exact fixture fixes interior t=3/5 and an explicit enlarged
L source tag, not the earlier K return contract. The rational
filters are D0=diag(2,1), D1=diag(1,2), with E=D^2/10.
The named U(c,d)=cI-i d X elements have pairs (3/5,4/5),
(5/13,12/13) and product (-33/65,56/65), with exact inverses.
Those labels are group elements, not additive times. Endpoint
scales are positive multiplicative parameters. General weighted
unitaries/generators, endpoint scaling, transpose, dephasing and
additional dominated effects are separate algebraic diagnostics,
not silently registered primary commands or physical availability.

**The proposed minimal completion and boundary classifications are proved.**
For each fixed known t in [-1,1], L_t is the native retwisting of four
independent diagonal blocks M_t(rho_ab), with each rho_ab positive and
the accepted reference/coupler convention held fixed. It is positively
isomorphic to a direct sum of four PSD2 cones and has real span dimension
16, including both pure-reference endpoints. Each block inverse is
`4 [Tr_ref(U† M_ab U)]^T`; exact membership reconstructs every native
entry, including off-block zeros. Complex transpose conventions and all
sixteen labels remain explicit.

Minimality is exact: any convex cone containing K_t and closed under
the four literal cuts contains each cut of an equal-block K_t source,
and summing four such cuts yields every independent L_t element.
Conversely L_t is convex, contains K_t and is cut-closed. This does not
assert physical independent-block preparations, mixtures or forgotten
selectors. The executable requires the explicit new L_t domain tag;
it is not an adapter silently changing the accepted K_t source contract.

Cell weights are `q_ab=Tr(E_ab rho_ab)` with
`E_ab=(I+(-1)^b tZ)/4`. Total-entry mass is their sum, whereas raw trace
is `tau=(1/4) sum_ab Tr(rho_ab)`. They agree at t=0 and on equal-block
K_t, but generally differ on L_t. Normalization and branch probabilities
always use total-entry mass. Mass here is not physical rest mass.

Literal cuts C_ab are positive on the entire new cone, calibrated and
raw-complete: `sum C_ab(N)=N`, `m C_ab=q_ab`, and
`C_beta C_alpha=delta(alpha,beta) C_alpha`. Positive-weight selected
cuts therefore perfectly repeat the FULL outcome, retaining both a and
b in every actual record. This succeeds by changing the range from K_t
to L_t; it does not contradict RI-08f or derive a physical reset law.
Neither another reference preparation nor another coupler application
is inferred after each cut.

For |t|<1, `(1-|t|) tau <= m <= (1+|t|) tau`, so the positive normalized
base is compact and mass faithful. At |t|=1, the following are distinct:

| Endpoint object in the 16-dimensional actual span | Dimension |
| --- | --- |
| Span of the positive zero-mass subcone | 4 |
| Common kernel of all whole-cone effects 0 <= e <= m | 12 |
| Kernel of the single total-mass functional | 15 |

Every whole-cone mass-dominated effect has unique block representatives
`0 <= F_ab <= E_ab`. At endpoints, rank-one E_ab forces
`F_ab=c_ab E_ab`, with coefficients in [0,1], so all such effects factor
through the four weights. The twelve-dimensional common kernel includes
coherence directions beyond the four-dimensional positive dark span.
Ordinary norm-bounded linear functionals are not the same class: raw
trace sees positive dark payload and fails domination by mass. At interior
t, mathematically dominated effects span all sixteen dual directions and
can distinguish coherence at equal cell weights; their availability is
not added to the cut-only catalogue.

The opposite-effect Z projector in one block, scaled by
`1/lambda_minus`, `lambda_minus=(1-|t|)/4`, has mass one and raw trace
`1/(1-|t|)`. It saturates the interior bound and diverges toward either
endpoint. At an endpoint, adding arbitrarily much positive dark payload
keeps mass unchanged. Thus compact-base constants are not uniform in t.

Endpoint zero-weight cuts may be NONZERO raw kernels. They remain
inspectable and cannot be normalized or committed. Raw completeness
includes them. Summing only normalized positive-probability outputs may
omit a nonzero raw remainder even while all scalar probabilities sum
correctly. No such remainder is called a zero matrix or silently removed
while claiming raw reconstruction.

For normalized sources at the SAME full prefix and known context, the
fixed full-cell/cut/stop catalogue has exact finite future-record
equivalence iff the four weights agree. Cut words descend to coordinate
projections; the immediate full read gives necessity. Finite known-history
stopping preserves the result. For unnormalized inputs, the analogous
claim concerns outcome-weight measures including total mass, not laws
conditioned on source normalization. Full raw residuals and records are
not replaced by this predictive summary. The positive weight section
establishes a faithful R_+^4 quotient algebraically, not an available reset.

RI-07's raw compact-base first-commit theorem applies at fixed interior
t only if its OTHER premises hold: fixed prefix/stationary experiment
or finite controller, complete positive mutually exclusive branch law,
finitely many full labels/output types, faithful committing-output masses,
and the required observation contract for discarded silent paths/counts.
The current typed catalogue supplies no new silent law. At endpoints the
raw theorem's faithfulness premise fails; the explicit faithful weight
quotient can support the theorem only after all claimed maps/effects
descend and the other premises hold. No convergence of raw operator sums
is inferred from scalar convergence, no nonexistent raw limit is used,
and no unbounded-history or uniform-parameter theorem is added.

**The candidate obstruction and tradeoff are proved conditionally.**
The study fixes the c=0 raw sixteen-label image K_t, the independent
reference `rho_t=(I+tZ)/2`, known Z orientation and accepted shared
coupler `U=(I tensor H) CNOT`, with `-1 <= t <= 1`. Native tensor labels
and their signed permutation/untwist are retained. The quotient image is
`conjugate(U) (rho tensor rho_t) transpose(U)`; the native block uses
`rho^T/2`, including complex input coherence. The real span has dimension
four. Its positive inverse, obtained by undoing the coupler and partially
tracing the reference, works at both singular endpoints. Membership
requires exact reconstruction of every native entry, not just a local
partial trace. On this image, total-entry mass, raw trace and Tr(rho)
agree; mass is normalization mass, not physical rest mass.

The four full-cell effects pull back to
`E_ab=(I+(-1)^b tZ)/4`, independent of a. Hence a normalized source has
full-outcome maximum `p_f=(1+|t|)/4 <= 1/2`. A general first-effect e /
next-question g lemma proves that every positive mass-calibrated branch
returning to the same cone has relative failure at least `1-beta`, where
beta is the normalized maximum of g. A normalized maximizing target h
gives algebraic attainment through `B=e h`. At the optimal uniform
relative bound, all positive outputs lie in the maximizing face. A ray
face fixes the branch uniquely on the entire span.

Applied to the fixed K_t, this gives the following distinct optima:

| Actual first branch / next question | Relative defect | Original-input-mass defect |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Fine (a,b) / same full (a,b) | `(3-|t|)/4` | `(1+|t|)(3-|t|)/16` |
| Fine (a,b) / same b | `(1-|t|)/2` | `(1-t^2)/8` |
| Analytically summed first b / same b | `(1-|t|)/2` | `(1-t^2)/4` |

The relative denominator is the actual first branch weight: q_ab in
the first two rows, q_b in the last. The last row sums separate labeled
histories; it is not permission to erase a or replace a known fine record
by a coarse one. The first and next effects need not be identical.

Thus perfectly repeating the full outcome is impossible in this same
image. Its relative defect is at least 1/2, strictly above 1/2 at interior
polarization. In contrast, b can repeat perfectly at |t|=1; next a remains
fair. A fresh next-question probability is not a deterministic reread of
the stored classical label. Both actual fine records remain intact.

For t nonzero, all four optimal branches are uniquely the calibrated
resets to the image of the aligned Z eigenprojector, with sign
`(-1)^b sign(t)`. The targets coincide across a at fixed b, not the
records. They simultaneously attain the fine and b optima. At t=0 all
positive calibrated branches are relatively optimal; `B_ab(N)=N/4` is
an exact non-reset counterexample to uniqueness. Choosing K_0 here does
not imply that RI-08d excluded c at t=0. An interior weighted-dephasing
counterexample also shows that the input-mass minimax optimum does not
force relative-optimal reset uniqueness.

The reusable fixture supplies additional same-image return operations
and target availability. It does not derive a physical reference-reset
procedure or reapply the shared coupler after every commitment. Nonzero
literal cell cuts have only one surviving block and cannot belong to an
image with four identical blocks. They are never treated as reusable
sources or targets. A zero-weight literal cut may be nonzero, whereas
the calibrated same-image algebraic branch is full zero and cannot be
normalized or appended.

The immutable executable retains both original typed inputs and their
full separate histories, named independence, fixed t/orientation,
coupler/frame/controller, native permutation, actual return setting,
every actual fine (a,b) outcome, and every original or previous joint
event as an origin-tagged precursor. Known changed settings remain
different. Analytical provenance alone does not invent a different
nominal action label. Fine/b query functions are read-only and distinct
from fresh commits. Source constructors check grammar and exact image,

**The domain-aware robustness obligation succeeds conditionally.**
The broader local-only cone C has full six-dimensional real span and
`D(A,c)` with `A >= |c| I`. If RI-08d's additional composite viability
premises are imposed, the primary surviving local cone is instead C0,
where c=0 and the real span has dimension four. Both use
`m(D)=2 Tr(A)=Tr(D)`, `Phi(D)=2 A^T` and `J2(rho)=D(rho^T/2,0)`.
Mass means normalization mass, not rest mass. The two premise sets are
not conflated.

On C0, every positive real-linear branch on the full Hermitian span with
the exact rank-one Pauli effect is `B(d)=e_Q(d) h_Q`, for one normalized
positive target h_Q. The proof uses faithful trace, the zero opposite-ray
image and positivity on arbitrary-coherence rank-one inputs to kill both
off-diagonal input images; it assumes neither Kraus form nor repeatability.
With an assumed uniform whole-cone repeatability defect at most epsilon,
where `0 <= epsilon <= 1/2`, the allowed targets are exactly the cap
`e_Q(h_Q) >= 1-epsilon`.
Calibration therefore classifies but does not uniquely choose the branch;
positive epsilon allows nonuniqueness. Same-domain range is explicit.

On either chosen domain, positivity, exact effect calibration and the
uniform defect imply the sharp RAW Hermitian trace-norm bound
`||B_tilde(d)-p J2(Q)||_1 <= 2 p sqrt(epsilon)`, with `p=e_Q(d)`.
This is not half trace distance. Zero p forces the entire raw output to
zero. On C, `|c_out| <= epsilon p/2` is separately sharp; C0 range forces
zero already. The raw norm is proved by all four eigenvalues of the
untwisted difference, not by replacing raw residuals with a quotient.
Equality with quotient distance is special to this matched-mass pure-ray
comparison, not general raw distance preservation. A pure tilt at
epsilon=9/25 attains norm 6/5; the diagonal epsilon=1/5 target attains
`|c|=1/10`. These two sharpness bounds cannot be jointly saturated for
positive epsilon in the stated range.

For the same full initial state, declared domain and full-history
controller, finite Clifford/Pauli protocols with at most N records,
including history-based early stopping, obey summed raw leaf-error bound
`min(2,2 N sqrt(epsilon))` and full-record total-variation bound
`min(1,(N-1) sqrt(epsilon))` for N>=1. Both errors vanish at N=0; the
first record law is exactly calibrated even when residuals differ.
The proof uses approximate positive prefixes and ideal signed-contractive
suffixes. It does not assume approximate complete positivity, signed
contraction or Phi-descent. The final update cannot change its own outcome
probabilities, yielding N-1 for record variation. A separate catalogue-
specific coupling argument improves the latter bound to
`1-(1-sqrt(epsilon(1-epsilon)))^(N-1)`; its two-record value is sharp,
without claiming an optimal general longer-horizon rate.

The larger local-only domain retains a non-descent counterexample:
epsilon=1/5, kappa=2/5 and input c feed the Z-positive output's visible
off-diagonal; its complement is the ideal Z-negative branch. Positivity
holds on all C because `|c_in| <= p/2`. Inputs A=I/4,c=+/-1/4 have
identical Phi but later X-positive conditional probabilities 9/10 and
1/10 (joint probabilities 9/20 and 1/20). The counterinstrument outputs
c=0, so its obstruction concerns the larger SOURCE domain. Such nonzero-c
inputs are excluded under C0/composite premises. The imaginary-c witness
independently checks the transpose convention with a later Y read.

Complete nominal setting identifiers, axes, outcomes, controller/frame,
domain, actual control words and full precursors are retained. Controls
act once; positive commitments clear only pending words. No zero branch
is normalized or appended. Approximate and ideal mathematical models of

**The proposed exclusion succeeds conditionally.** Start with the full
local `D(A,c)`, `A >= |c| I`, and the independently declared normalized
reference `D((I+tZ)/4,0)`. No c=0 input restriction is imposed. Native
tensor labels `(a,i,b,j)` and regrouped `(a,b,i,j)` are connected by an
explicit signed permutation; all sixteen raw labels and all complex
cross-cell entries remain intact. Initially every admitted local c gives
a positive, normalized, exactly recordable independent product.

The supplied shared-system lift of `(I tensor H) CNOT`, with the first
system controlling the second, has
`U(I tensor B)U†=(I+t Z tensor X)/4`. Its forward cross-cell forms are
`Gamma(00,10)=tc` and `Gamma(01,11)=-tc`, with conjugate reverses and all
other off-cell forms zero. For real t≠0, exact complex recordability forces
c=0, including its imaginary part. Conversely every positive A with c=0
survives with full PSD and mass `4 Tr(A) Tr(B)=2 Tr(A)`. The maximal
surviving LOCAL subcone relative to this fixed interface is exactly the
c=0 section. At t=0 the entire local cone survives.

The prospective invalid c≠0 output of this particular coupler still has
mass one: tc and -tc cancel. Positivity, ordinary trace, total mass and
diagonal weights alone therefore miss the failure. No illegal output is
promoted, normalized or cut as a lawful source. This is interface-domain
exclusion, not a physical measurement of c or a dynamics setting it to zero.

Countercontrols are universal in their stated algebraic scopes. Any local
product unitary leaves the off-a read contraction zero. At t=0 the
reference cross operator is central (`I4/4`), so any shared unitary in
this same interface is blind. CNOT alone is also blind; its final target H
is significant. All c=0 inputs survive every shared unitary algebraically,
but their availability is not inferred. SWAP illustrates why general
shared controls need not preserve total-entry mass off that domain:

**The proposed classification succeeds conditionally.** A general
finite-dimensional exposed-ray lemma proves that a positive real-linear
branch with mass effect e and perfect repeatability has the unique form
`B(d)=e(d)h` whenever the positive repeat face is the normalized ray h.
The cone must span the actual vector space, and the branch requirements
apply to the entire cone, not merely tested or reachable preparations.

For the full RI-08b six-dimensional raw cone, `e_Q(d)=m(d)` forces
`Phi(d)=m(d)Q`. Then `A=m(d)Q^T/2` has a null direction, so the raw
positivity constraint `A >= |c| I` forces c=0. The exposed face is exactly
the ray J2(Q). Existence and uniqueness therefore give
`B_Q(d)=Tr(Phi(d)Q) J2(Q)` on the whole signed span. Initial complex c is
retained; its output removal is proved, not an input truncation. The
rank-one quotient identity `Phi(B_Q d)=Q rho Q` is derived after the
classification, not inserted as a Kraus/Lüders premise.

Positive outcomes normalize to J2(Q); zero-effect inputs map to the entire
zero raw kernel, and no zero branch is normalized or committed. Same-axis
branches are idempotent and mutually annihilating. Finite products and
record-dependent feedback preserve the source cone and complete probability
accounting. Convex-mixture branch laws and posterior reweighting are explicit.
The actual control word acts once on the current residual in the fixed
frame; commitment stores axis/outcome/word/controller/type/frame and full
precursor, clearing only the pending word. A repeat does not secretly
reapply or undo the earlier control. Full immutable earlier records survive.

Countermodels locate the premises: with the same effects and positive
source range, a mixed-state reset is not repeatable; with an explicitly
extended target cell effect and larger K_P range, the old literal cut has
the same source probabilities and repeatability but a different raw output.
It can retain a nonzero zero-mass raw branch. It remains separately typed,
and the new instrument provides no return/reset map on that terminal target.

**Preparation reachability stays restricted.** Starting at J2(I/2), full
record-conditioned histories with Clifford controls and Pauli outcomes reach
exactly the center plus six Pauli eigenstates. Explicit finite preparation
randomization gives residual marginals in exactly the stabilizer octahedron,
`|x|+|y|+|z|<=1`; real weights give the full closed octahedron and rational
weights its rational-coordinate points. Known selectors and all component
records remain in a joint ensemble, not erased into a single State. This
is not a predictive quotient for selector-aware feedback. The valid Bloch
state `(3/5,0,3/5)` lies outside the octahedron, and source c≠0 cannot be
generated from this c=0 initial preparation. Full mathematical state-space
tomography does not supply full physical preparation richness.

### Remaining obligation and halt

The next substantive obligation is selection/justification of an actual
preparation, control and observation interface if the theorem is to support a
physical or applied model. A residual reduction must be justified against all
of that interface's continuations. General nonfaithful-domain classification,
global histories and uniform limits are not solved by this bounded result.
A further theorem or application needs its own coordinator assignment.

The QR owner stops here for acceptance. No successor implementation, completed-
bundle rewrite, RET, clock, book, QR-05 atlas or physical promotion follows;
Option B and Status M remain unchanged.

## Bounded result and exact ownership

The [exact-recordability domain study](../validation/t8-q-record-context-domain-2026-09-13/CONTEXT_DOMAIN.md)
showed that two initially recordable contexts need not remain mutually legal
after a selective cut. The present candidate chooses smaller, explicitly
encoded rational preparation cones and a controlled interaction between them.
It retains the residual state, mode, complete committed labels and precursor
rule; an unavailable context action is refused, not renormalized.

New bundle:
[CONTROLLED_CONTEXT.md](../validation/t8-q-controlled-context-2026-09-13/CONTROLLED_CONTEXT.md),
[model.py](../validation/t8-q-controlled-context-2026-09-13/model.py),
[check.py](../validation/t8-q-controlled-context-2026-09-13/check.py).
The only other authored changes in this assignment are this handoff and the
two QR map/plan files linked above. Older uncommitted QR changes, concurrent
core/RET changes and the coordinator's changes are not included in that claim.

The model instantiates finite typed future-effect closure, returning a lawful
distinguishing experiment when two residual states are not equivalent. It also
proves that the chosen switch has no positive linear extension to the full
recordability cone. Its fixed read–switch–read program has recorded probability
`Pr(r,t) = x_(r,r xor t)` and separates all four coordinates. Predictive rank is
four in each mode: there is **no nontrivial residual compression** here.

Repeated attempts have first-commit probabilities `2^(-n-1) x_(r,0)` and
never-commit mass `x_(0,1) + x_(1,1)`. An attempt-only quotient loses a
distinction recoverable by the added read action; the complete catalogue's
quotient has zero kernel. The declared product is classical and limited.

## Proof review and scope corrections incorporated

The implementation and independent test suite were written by separate agents.
A third agent reconstructed the cone, closure, equivalence, silent-limit and
quotient arguments independently, then read the full note. The coordinator
also supplied an independent preview. These are internal reviews, not external
peer review or a proof-assistant certificate.

The reviews led to explicit qualifications in the final source/note:

- State/history validation checks chain grammar and forward closure, not
  global reachability of an arbitrary supplied residual/history pair.
- The structural `append_record` helper requires an already selected positive
  branch; only state-aware `apply_branch` checks its probability before append.
- Procedure-boundary no-append observations are not additional immutable
  records or physical timestamps. The stronger read–switch–read separation
  uses two actual committed outcomes without stochastic-silent timing access.
- The general finite-effect theorem uses a finite terminal-test list, tagged
  terminal observations, and rational maps/effects/compared states for exact
  decidability. The executable implements only this two-mode, single-terminal-
  mass instance, not a general process SDK.
- Tensor-local residual-map commutation does not prove whole-history schedule
  covariance: the selected global append rule builds a chain.
- Generator identities extend to all positive inputs by linearity; typed
  final-span invariance and the universal geometric-series first-commit limits
  are proved in the note, not inferred from a finite sample of tests.

An iterable-consumption issue in the early `record_weights` implementation was
found and fixed before final checking. No other implementation defect remained
in the reviewed scope. No unresolved algebraic blocker was reported.
